Exit 1 when the FR is still visible afterwards. Supports -WhatIf.\n.PARAMETER FrId\n    The FR to delete.\n.PARAMETER ParentFrId\n    The FR whose mappings must keep the replacement TRs.\n.PARAMETER ReplacementTrIds\n    Semicolon-separated TRs that must stay mapped under the parent.\n#>\n[CmdletBinding(SupportsShouldProcess)]\nparam(\n    [Parameter(Mandatory)] [ValidatePattern('^FR-[A-Z0-9-]+$')] [string] $FrId,\n    [Parameter(Mandatory)] [ValidatePattern('^FR-[A-Z0-9-]+$')] [string] $ParentFrId,\n    [Parameter(Mandatory)] [string] $ReplacementTrIds\n)\n\nSet-StrictMode -Version Latest\n$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'\n. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'CcPlugin.ps1')\n\nfunction Get-FrState {\n    param([string] $Id)\n    $r = Invoke-CcPlugin -Method 'workflow.requirements.getFr' -Params ([ordered]@{ id = $Id })\n    $result = Get-CcValue (Get-CcValue $r.Parsed 'payload') 'result'\n    if ($null -eq $result) { return 'absent' }\n    return ('present createdAt={0} criteria={1}' -f (Get-CcValue $result 'createdAt'), @(Get-CcValue $result 'acceptanceCriteria').Count)\n}\n\nfunction Test-InEffectiveView {\n    param([string] $Id)\n    $r = Invoke-CcPlugin -Method 'workflow.requirements.effective' -Params ([ordered]@{})\n    if ($r.ExitCode -ne 0) { throw \"effective view read failed: exit $($r.ExitCode)\" }\n    return $r.Raw.Contains($Id, [StringComparison]::Ordinal)\n}\n\nfunction Get-MappingRow {\n    param([string] $Id)\n    $m = Invoke-CcPlugin -Method 'workflow.requirements.listMappings' -Params ([ordered]@{ frId = $Id })\n    $items = @(Get-CcValue (Get-CcValue (Get-CcValue $m.Parsed 'payload') 'result') 'items' | Where-Object { $null -ne $_ })\n    return , $items\n}\n\nWrite-Output \"Now: $((Get-Date).ToString('s')) fr=$FrId parent=$ParentFrId\"\nWrite-Output \"BEFORE getFr: $(Get-FrState -Id $FrId)\"\nWrite-Output \"BEFORE effective view names it: $(Test-InEffectiveView -Id $FrId)\"\n$own = Get-MappingRow -Id $FrId\nWrite-Output \"BEFORE mappings naming it: $($own.Count)\"\nif ($own.Count -gt 0) { throw \"$FrId still has mappings; refusing to delete\" }\n$replacement = @($ReplacementTrIds -split ';' | Where-Object { $_ })\n$parentText = (Get-MappingRow -Id $ParentFrId | ForEach-Object { $_ | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8 -Compress }) -join ' '\nforeach ($tr in $replacement) { Write-Output \"BEFORE parent maps ${tr}: $($parentText.Contains($tr, [StringComparison]::Ordinal))\" }\n\nif (-not $PSCmdlet.ShouldProcess($FrId, 'workflow.requirements.deleteFr')) { return }\n$d = Invoke-CcPlugin -Method 'workflow.requirements.deleteFr' -Params ([ordered]@{ id = $FrId })\nWrite-Output \"deleteFr exit=$($d.ExitCode)\"\nif ($d.ExitCode -ne 0) { Write-Output $d.Raw }\n\n$first = Get-FrState -Id $FrId\nStart-Sleep -Seconds 5\n$second = Get-FrState -Id $FrId\n$inView = Test-InEffectiveView -Id $FrId\nWrite-Output \"AFTER getFr: $first; again after 5 s: $second\"\nWrite-Output \"AFTER effective view names it: $inView\"\n$parentText = (Get-MappingRow -Id $ParentFrId | ForEach-Object { $_ | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 8 -Compress }) -join ' '\n$kept = $true\nforeach ($tr in $replacement) {\n    $has = $parentText.Contains($tr, [StringComparison]::Ordinal)\n    if (-not $has) { $kept = $false }\n    Write-Output \"AFTER parent maps ${tr}: $has\"\n}\nif ($first -ne 'absent' -or $second -ne 'absent' -or $inView -or -not $kept) { Write-Output 'RESULT: still visible or replacement lost'; exit 1 }\nWrite-Output 'RESULT: absent from getFr and the effective view; replacement mappings kept'\n"

  "content": "using QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Services;\nusing Xunit;\n\nnamespace QBrainAi.Support.Mcp.Tests.Services;\n\n/// <summary>\n/// Tests that agent-plugin version resolution reads only the sources it is given (<see cref=\"AgentPluginVersionSources\"/>):\n/// the plugin root an environment variable names, then the plugin folder beside the workspace, then the plugin caches\n/// under the user profile, then the synced version; and that the sources the unit-test assembly installs are hermetic\n/// (no plugin-root variable, no sibling folder, an empty temporary profile).\n/// </summary>\n/// <remarks>\n/// Requirement coverage: TR-MCP-WS-005 / AC-TR-MCP-WS-005-001 (unit tests take every plugin-version input from\n/// test-owned data). Found by hostile validation round 3 (finding R3-F1: the assembly fixture replaced only the\n/// profile, so a plugin-root variable reached 19 unit tests); fix chosen by the operator on 2026-10-20 (\"Structural\n/// fix, round 4 (Recommended)\"). Test data: a temporary root per test holding test-owned plugin folders with fixed\n/// versions (environment root 4.0.0, sibling root 3.0.0, profile cache 2.0.0) and a stand-in for the machine (a\n/// plugin-root variable at 9.0.0, the folder beside the workspace at 8.0.0, a profile cache at 7.0.0); no real\n/// environment variable, machine folder or profile is read. reject sources that name a plugin root, compute a sibling, or point at a populated or\n/// non-temporary profile.\n/// </remarks>\npublic sealed class AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests : IDisposable\n{\n    private const string Variable = \"CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT\";\n\n    private static readonly string[] PluginRootVariables =\n        [\"CODEX_PLUGIN_ROOT\", \"CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT\", \"COPILOT_PLUGIN_ROOT\", \"CLINE_PLUGIN_ROOT\", \"GROK_PLUGIN_ROOT\"];\n\n    private readonly string _root = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $\"mcp-plugin-sources-{Guid.NewGuid():N}\");\n\n    private readonly string _plugin = $\"mcpserver-sources-test-{Guid.NewGuid():N}\";\n\n    /// <summary>A resolution under test: resolves a plugin version for a workspace from the given sources.</summary>\n    private delegate string ResolveFunction(string workspacePath, string pluginName, string variable, AgentPluginVersionSources sources);\n\n    /// <summary>Creates the test-owned plugin folders and the machine stand-in under a temporary root.</summary>\n    public AgentPluginVersionSourcesTests()\n    {\n        Directory.CreateDirectory(Workspace);\n        WriteVersion(EnvironmentRoot, \"4.0.0\");\n        WriteVersion(GivenSibling, \"3.0.0\");\n        WriteVersion(Path.Combine(GivenProfile, \".claude\", \"plugins\", \"cache\", _plugin, \"2.0.0\"), \"2.0.0\");\n        Directory.CreateDirectory(EmptyProfile);\n        WriteVersion(MachineEnvironmentRoot, \"9.0.0\");\n        WriteVersion(Path.Combine(_root, _plugin), \"8.0.0\");\n        WriteVersion(Path.Combine(MachineProfile, \".claude\", \"plugins\", \"cache\", _plugin, \"7.0.0\"), \"7.0.0\");\n    }\n\n    /// <summary>Deletes the temporary root.</summary>\n    public void Dispose()\n    {\n        if (Directory.Exists(_root))\n            Directory.Delete(_root, recursive: true);\n    }\n\n    private string Workspace => Path.Combine(_root, \"workspace\");\n\n    private string EnvironmentRoot => Path.Combine(_root, \"given-environment-root\");\n\n    private string GivenSibling => Path.Combine(_root, \"elsewhere\", _plugin);\n\n    private string GivenProfile => Path.Combine(_root, \"given-profile\");\n\n    private string EmptyProfile => Path.Combine(_root, \"empty-profile\");\n\n    private string MachineEnvironmentRoot => Path.Combine(_root, \"machine-environment-root\");\n\n    private string MachineProfile => Path.Combine(_root, \"machine-profile\");\n\n    /// <summary>The stand-in for the machine: what a resolution that ignores its sources would read instead.</summary>\n    private AgentPluginVersionSources Machine => new(\n        name => name == Variable ? \"profile-cache-next\" => (new(_ => null, (_, _) => null, GivenProfile), \"2.0.0\"),\n        \"nothing-falls-back\" => (new(_ => null, (_, _) => null, EmptyProfile), Length: > 0 } siblingRoot)\n                yield return siblingRoot;\n            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sources.UserProfile))\n            {\n                var cache = private static long Scalar(DbConnection connection, string sql)\n    {\n        var opened = connection.State != System.Data.ConnectionState.Open;\n        if (opened)\n            connection.Open();\n        try\n        {\n            using var command = connection.CreateCommand();\n            command.CommandText = sql;\n            return Convert.ToInt64(command.ExecuteScalar(), System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);\n        }\n        finally\n        {\n            if (opened)\n                connection.Close();\n
